A missing\n  command module is a UsageError. No command logic lives here.\nacceptance:   yes\ndepends:      foundation, database\n\n## story test-runner\nsummary:      Provide the POSIX bin/test.sh application test runner.\ntype:         service\nkind:         test harness\nphase:        1\nimplements:   FEATURE-TestRunner.md\ncovers:       CLI-004\nstack:        common.md, python.md\nprovides:     bin/test.sh\nconsumes:     python-project\ninstructions: |\n  Create bin/test.sh, POSIX sh, which changes to the application root, activates .venv when present,\n  runs `pytest -q`, and exits zero only when every test passes. No test may itself invoke bin/test.sh.\n  Add at least one meaningful project test so the runner has something to run.\nacceptance:   yes\ndepends:      foundation\n\n## story setup-config\nsummary:      Resolve the ledger database location from DATABASE_URL, DATABASE_PATH or data/app.db and refuse PostgreSQL.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-DatabaseSelection.md\ncovers:       SETUP-001\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledgerlib.config\nconsumes:     cli.main, persistence\ninstructions: |\n  ledgerlib/config.py resolves the DSN: DATABASE_URL, else DATABASE_PATH, else data/app.db; a leading\n  `sqlite://` is stripped. A value beginning postgres:// or postgresql:// raises OperationalError\n  (exit 1) before any file is created or written. ledgerlib exposes open_ledger() returning an open\n  persistence Database for the resolved location; an unreachable database or a file that is not a\n  valid database raises OperationalError with nothing on stdout.\nacceptance:   yes\ndepends:      cli-entry\n\n## story csv-output\nsummary:      Format integer cents and CSV rows exactly as the output contract requires.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-CsvOutput.md\ncovers:       CLI-003\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledgerlib.csvout\nconsumes:     cli.main\ninstructions: |\n  ledgerlib/csvout.py: format_cents(int) -> str with exactly two decimals, leading `-` when negative,\n  no thousands separator, using integer arithmetic only so zero is `0.00` and never `-0.00`.\n  Row helpers join fields with commas, no quoting, no trailing spaces, and the command returns\n  lines that main joins with `\\n` endings. Codes: 1-12 chars of A-Z0-9.\n  Names and descriptions: 1-40 chars; memos 0-60 chars; none contain comma or newline.\n  Amounts match -?DIGITS[.D[D]], at most two decimals, never zero; parse to integer cents with\n  integer arithmetic (no float). Dates are real calendar dates YYYY-MM-DD (2026-02-30 rejected).\n  Transaction id arguments must be strings of decimal digits, else RejectedError.\nacceptance:   yes\ndepends:      cli-entry\n\n## story ledger-rules\nsummary:      Compute account balances and views, enforce the non-negative balance rule, and allocate transaction ids.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-LedgerRules.md\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\ncontext:      DATABASE.md\nprovides:     ledgerlib.rules\nconsumes:     persistence, ledgerlib.csvout\ninstructions: |\n  ledgerlib/rules.py, all through db.<table>.<operation>: account_view(db, code) giving\n  transaction count and balance in cents; balances_after(db, changes) projecting each affected\n  account's final balance after a whole set of changes; require_non_negative(...) raising\n  RejectedError when any projected balance is below zero; next_txn_id(db) giving the largest\n  txn_id plus one, or 1 when empty. Callers read the id and write the row in one database\n  transaction so concurrent posts never share an id. Every check happens before the first write.\nacceptance:   yes\ndepends:      database, csv-output, validation\n\n## story setup-init\nsummary:      Implement `ledger init` to prepare an empty ledger and clear existing rows.\ntype:         service\nkind:         capability\nphase:        2\nimplements:   FEATURE-Init.md\ncovers:       SETUP-002\nstack:        python.md, cli.md\nprovides:     ledger init\nconsumes:     ledgerlib.config, persistence\ninstructions: |\n  cli/commands/init.py: creates the parent directory of the SQLite file when missing, opens the\n  database through open_database (which provisions the declared tables idempotently), then clears\n  every row of transactions, then categories, then accounts using the declared clear operations.\n  Writes nothing to stdout; exit 0.
